- **q-a-tester.agent**: Use when: developer needs minimal test cases created. Trigger phrases: test, validate, verify, check functionality, smoke test, sanity check, integration test. Creates 1-2 essential test cases (Python/JS/HTML/CSS/Markdown) with assertions, generates executable validation script, and runs tests immediately to confirm specification compliance. ([rubenvr-commits/yfinance-agentic-workflow](https://github.com/rubenvr-commits/yfinance-agentic-workflow/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/q-a-tester.agent.md))
- **q-authority-auditor**: Read-only auditor of the q/Python authority boundary: q owns the domain (schemas, business keys, transforms, job and dataset declarations, which producer writes which destination, lifecycle and conflict semantics, the rules that refuse bad data), Python owns the CLI, deployment, orchestration and reporting, and consumes q's domain rather than restating or inventing it. Hunts six defects - duplicated, inverted and bypassed authority, missing declarations, stale projections, and specification gaps - in code, diffs, issues and designs, and above all checks that a rule that matters is enforced on… ([kwojdalski/uqf](https://github.com/kwojdalski/uqf/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/q-authority-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **q-coder**: Coder role of the q-workflow. It implements an approved plan in the Agaton repos, follows the existing conventions, gets all format, lint, type and test checks green, commits locally on the guarded task branch, and fixes or rebuts q-reviewer findings. It is spawned by the q-impl, q-plan-impl and q-pr-resolve skills. ([tranquan/qstack](https://github.com/tranquan/qstack/blob/HEAD/claudecode/agents/q-coder.md))
- **q-commerce-ops**: Usa per micro-fulfillment e dark store — picking, stoccaggio, layout del magazzino, tempi di preparazione ordine, inventario in tempo reale per la consegna rapida.
